- **android-vivo-calculator**: 在 vivo 计算器（com.android.bbkcalculator）里做四则运算、读计算结果、算汇率或翻历史记录时使用。典型场景：帮我算一笔账、这个折扣多少钱、汇率换算。触发词：计算器、算一下、等于多少、汇率。 不适用：Agent 自己就能算的四则运算与百分比（口算/直接算出答案更快，开应用反而多一步且有读屏误差）；复杂科学计算。 ([Luwi-developers/app-skills](https://github.com/Luwi-developers/app-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/android-vivo-calculator))
- **android-vivo-calendar**: 在 vivo 日历（com.bbk.calendar）里新建日程、搜索/修改/删除已有日程、看某天的安排、切视图或看课程表时使用。典型场景：把课表或会议写进日历、查询某天日程、补建一次性提醒、改一条日程的时间。触发词：加日程、写进日历、安排提醒、查日历、明天有什么安排、课程表。 不适用：把教务课表批量同步进日历课程表（用 campus-schedule-sync）；结合课表与天气排一天行程（用 weather-trip-planning）；读系统日历数据库（那是 lw_calendar 工具，不是本技能）。 ([Luwi-developers/app-skills](https://github.com/Luwi-developers/app-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/android-vivo-calendar))
- **android-vivo-camera**: 在 vivo 相机（com.android.camera）里拍照、录像、切模式、切前后摄、调变焦/闪光灯/画幅/倒计时，或改相机设置时使用。典型场景：拍下眼前的东西、录一段视频、切前置、调画幅与倒计时。触发词：拍照、拍一下、录像、前置、变焦、画幅、倒计时、相机设置。 不适用：识别镜头前是什么（用 lw_look 或视频模式，不必开相机应用）；对已拍照片做编辑（用 dsh-imagegen-edit）；找相册里的照片（用 android-vivo-gallery）。 ([Luwi-developers/app-skills](https://github.com/Luwi-developers/app-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/android-vivo-camera))
- **android-vivo-clock**: 在 vivo 闹钟时钟（com.android.BBKClock）里查看/新建/启停闹钟、看某个时区的当前时间、操作秒表与计时器时使用。典型场景：明早几点叫我、把 6 点的闹钟关掉、看现在纽约几点、帮我计个时。触发词：闹钟、叫我起床、定个提醒、世界时钟、计时器、秒表。 不适用：只想用一句自然语言让系统助手顺手设个闹钟（用 android-vivo-copilot 一步到位）；想按日期提醒某件事（用 android-vivo-calendar 建日程）。 ([Luwi-developers/app-skills](https://github.com/Luwi-developers/app-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/android-vivo-clock))
- **android-vivo-copilot**: 用蓝心小V（com.vivo.ai.copilot）以文字下系统指令、问手机操作问题、让它代做设置类任务或找智能体时使用。典型场景：设闹钟/开热点/调亮度这类系统设置交给他办、问「怎么清后台」、看有哪些智能体。触发词：蓝心小V、问小V、让小V设闹钟、系统助手、智能体广场、深度研究。 不适用：Agent 自己用 lw_* 工具就能一遍做完的操作（绕道小V更慢且结果要再验证）；账号、支付、验证码类指令（一律不代发）；只是想设个闹钟/日程（直接用 android-vivo-clock / android-vivo-calendar 更可控）。 ([Luwi-developers/app-skills](https://github.com/Luwi-developers/app-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/android-vivo-copilot))
- **android-vivo-filemanager**: 在 vivo 文件管理（com.android.filemanager）里按分类找文件、搜索文件名、看存储占用、进具体目录、找最近删除时使用。典型场景：找下载的安装包、看还剩多少空间、找被删的文件、确认某个文件在哪。触发词：文件管理、找文件、下载目录、存储空间、最近删除。 不适用：Agent 用 read / glob / bash 就能直接读写或列出的路径（自己处理即可）；删除文件与空间清理（不可逆，只指路）；只想知道剩余空间（用 lw_storage 更快）。 ([Luwi-developers/app-skills](https://github.com/Luwi-developers/app-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/android-vivo-filemanager))
- **android-vivo-gallery**: 在 vivo 相册（com.vivo.gallery）里浏览照片、进某个相册、看单张照片的信息或工具栏、找回收站（最近删除）时使用。典型场景：找某张截图、看某个相册有多少张、把删掉的照片找回来、看照片的拍摄信息。触发词：相册、找照片、翻相册、最近删除、回收站、这张照片的信息。 不适用：改图修图抠图去水印（用 dsh-imagegen-edit）；只想看屏幕上现在是什么（用屏幕截图工具，不进相册）；清理建议与一键清理（不可逆，只指路）。 ([Luwi-developers/app-skills](https://github.com/Luwi-developers/app-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/android-vivo-gallery))

- **android-vivo-notes**: 在 vivo 原子笔记（com.android.notes）里新建/追加/查找笔记、管理待办与文档、建文件夹或找回最近删除时使用。典型场景：把整理好的长文存进指定笔记、每日往同一笔记追加一行、在文件夹间归档、勾掉一条待办。触发词：记笔记、存进笔记、原子笔记、往笔记里加一段、建文件夹、新建待办、笔记里的文档。 不适用：往「健康日报」那类有专门流程的单篇笔记里写数据（用 health-daily-report）；需要账号协作的云笔记（本机没有）。 ([Luwi-developers/app-skills](https://github.com/Luwi-developers/app-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/android-vivo-notes))

- **android-vivo-settings**: 在 vivo 系统设置（com.android.settings）里查询与修改系统开关、找某个设置项在哪、进二级设置页时使用。典型场景：查/开蓝牙、看还剩多少存储、找某个开关藏在哪一页。触发词：设置、开蓝牙、飞行模式、系统设置、存储还剩多少、设置项在哪。 不适用：亮度、屏幕超时、自动旋转、字体大小这类本机有 lw_system / lw_net 能直接读写的（不必进设置页）；恢复出厂、清数据、改锁屏密码、账户解绑（只报入口路径，不代点）；某个应用自己的设置（进那个应用）。 ([Luwi-developers/app-skills](https://github.com/Luwi-developers/app-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/android-vivo-settings))

- **anf-feature-cycle**: ANF 클론에 ANF 기능 하나를 추가하는 전체 사이클을 조율한다. 사이클은 조사, 설계 상의, 구현, WSL 실환경 검증, 개념 문서 순서다. "스냅샷 기능 만들어줘", "용량 풀 구현하자", "다음 기능 진행", "export policy 추가"처럼 ANF 기능을 구현·추가·확장하자는 요청에 사용한다. "스냅샷 재검증해줘", "볼륨 개념 문서만 다시 써줘", "조사부터 다시", "이전 결과 바탕으로 보완", "결과 개선" 같은 후속 요청에도 사용한다. 남은 기능 여러 개를 이어서 끝까지 맡기는 요청("남은 기능 끝까지 진행해", "로드맵 진행해")에는 anf-roadmap을 사용한다. 기능과 무관한 단순 질문, 버그 하나 수정, 환경 설정 질문에는 사용하지 않는다. ([goldenbit1999/anf-clone](https://github.com/goldenbit1999/anf-clone/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/anf-feature-cycle))
- **anf-framework**: Implements the Assemble-Normalize-Fill (ANF) component workflow for building UI sections. Use this skill when building any new page section, landing page, or component that requires matching a design. Trigger words: "build a section," "create a page," "implement the design," "build the hero," or any UI construction task after brand extraction is complete. ([sebassfc/claude-code](https://github.com/sebassfc/claude-code/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/anf-framework))
- **anf-research**: ANF 클론에서 구현할 ANF 기능 하나가 실제 Azure NetApp Files와 ONTAP에서 어떻게 동작하는지 조사해 조사 브리프를 쓰는 절차다. anf-researcher 에이전트가 따른다. "ANF 스냅샷은 실제로 어떻게 동작해?", "용량 풀 API 스펙 조사해줘", "export policy가 ONTAP에서는 뭐야"처럼 구현하지 않고 ANF·ONTAP의 동작만 확인하려는 요청에 사용한다. 기능 구현을 전제로 한 조사("스냅샷 기능 조사부터 시작하자")는 anf-feature-cycle을 사용한다. ANF와 무관한 일반 주제 조사나 출처 검증 보고서에는 사용하지 않는다. ([goldenbit1999/anf-clone](https://github.com/goldenbit1999/anf-clone/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/anf-research))
- **anf-roadmap**: ANF 클론의 남은 기능(FR)을 FR-13까지 순서대로 끝까지 완성하는 총괄 절차다. 한 세션이 FR마다 anf-feature-cycle을 돌리고, 조사·스파이크·구현·검증·문서는 서브에이전트에 맡기며, 기능이 끝날 때마다 PR을 merge하고 다음 FR로 넘어간다. "남은 기능 끝까지 진행해", "FR-13까지 알아서 완성해", "로드맵 진행해", "나머지 기능 다 만들어줘"처럼 여러 기능을 이어서 맡기는 요청에 사용한다. "로드맵 이어서", "총괄 재개", "로드맵 멈춘 데서 계속", "자동 채택한 결정 보여줘" 같은 후속 요청에도 사용한다. 기능 하나만 진행하거나 다시 하는 요청("다음 기능 진행해", "스냅샷 재검증해줘")에는 anf-feature-cycle을 쓰고, 단순 질문·버그 하나 수정·환경 설정에는 쓰지 않는다. ([goldenbit1999/anf-clone](https://github.com/goldenbit1999/anf-clone/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/anf-roadmap))
- **anf-website-builder**: Build professional websites that don't look AI-generated using the ANIF Framework (Assemble, Normalize, Illustrate, Fill). Use when the user wants a designer-made-looking website, landing page, or marketing page. Triggers: 'build me a website', 'create a landing page', 'ANF framework'. Do NOT use for: dashboards, admin panels, web apps with complex state, or internal tools — those need different patterns. ([ErikTMA/claude-plugins](https://github.com/ErikTMA/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/anf-website-builder/skills/anf-website-builder))

- **anfechtung-142-und-rueckabwicklung**: Nutze diesen Skill, wenn eine wirksame Anfechtung den Rechtsgrund rückwirkend beseitigt. Normen: §§ 119 bis 124 BGB sowie §§ 142 und 812 BGB. Prüfraster: Prüfe das Spezialrecht vor dem allgemeinen Bereicherungsrecht; Übernimm Schutzwertungen in Saldo, Wertersatz und Entreicherung; Trenne Rückabwicklung, Schadensersatz und öffentlich-rechtliche Erstattung. Output: Anfechtungs- und Rückabwicklungsmatrix mit Anspruchszielen. Abgrenzung: nicht Rücktritt §§ 346 ff. BGB. ([Koftakhor/germanlegal](https://github.com/Koftakhor/germanlegal/tree/HEAD/bereicherungs-und-anfechtungsrecht-pruefer/skills/anfechtung-142-und-rueckabwicklung) · ★1)

- **anforderung**: Legt eine Anforderung (User Story mit Akzeptanzkriterien) in docs/anforderungen/ des Projekts an. Verwenden, wenn der Nutzer eine Anforderung, User Story, Akzeptanzkriterien, ein Epic-Detail oder das Ergebnis aus dem Refinement dokumentieren will, oder Begriffe wie "Anforderung", "User Story", "Acceptance Criteria", "Akzeptanzkriterium", "Requirement" nennt. ([patrickwerner99/-HSAA_ProgSem_WS2627_Gruppe02](https://github.com/patrickwerner99/-HSAA_ProgSem_WS2627_Gruppe02/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skills/anforderung) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **anforderungen**: Interaktion 1 von 3 im Sprint-Prozess. Der Mensch erklärt Ziele in seinen Worten; dieser Skill macht daraus messbare, testbare Anforderungen mit stabiler A-ID und je einem Testfall und schreibt sie direkt in anforderungen.md und testfaelle.md des Projekts. Nutzen bei "Anforderungen aufschreiben", "was soll das Tool können", "mach daraus Anforderungen", "/anforderungen". Nicht verwenden, um einen Sprint zu schneiden (sprint) oder um zu bauen oder zu testen. ([labiotanks-hash/ki-buero-automation](https://github.com/labiotanks-hash/ki-buero-automation/tree/HEAD/skills/anforderungen))
- **anforderungen**: Macht aus Gesprächen mit zahlenden Erstkunden eine Liste, was gebaut wird und was nicht: Anforderungen mit Quelle, Häufigkeit und Zahlungsbereitschaft, ein kleinster sinnvoller Umfang und ein Plan. Teil des Erstkunden-Skills. Nutze den Skill bei „was soll ich bauen“, „Anforderungen sammeln“, „Kundenfeedback auswerten“, „MVP festlegen“, „Feature-Liste priorisieren“. ([Ultra-Smashed/erstkunden-skill](https://github.com/Ultra-Smashed/erstkunden-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/anforderungen))
- **anforderungsdokument**: WAMOCON Entwicklungsprompts: Tiefenanalyse, Marketing/UX-Rework und Anforderungsdokument (9 Kapitel + Quellenverzeichnis als .docx). Workflow: IDEA.md ausfüllen, Skill aufrufen, Dokument prüfen, Freigabe. ([Wamocon/damicon](https://github.com/Wamocon/damicon/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/anforderungsdokument))
- **anforderungspaket-formulierer**: Granulare Anforderungspakete pro Subsystem formulieren, aus bestehenden Anforderungen — inklusive Niveau-C-Extrakt. - **anforderungsprofil-konstitutiv**: Für Anforderungsprofil — konstitutiv oder deklaratorisch: ordnet Norm, Beweislast und Gegenargument; Ergebnis: Prüfprodukt mit Risiko und nächstem Schritt. ([ThomasMoreAI/legal-skills-open](https://github.com/ThomasMoreAI/legal-skills-open/tree/HEAD/de/administrative/skills/anforderungsprofil-konstitutiv) · ★136)

- **anfra-aml**: Write and edit AML — the declarative modeling language for models, fields, relationships, and datasets in an AMQL repo. Use whenever you need to create or change a model, dataset, relationship, or field definition. ([holistics/anfra](https://github.com/holistics/anfra/tree/HEAD/skills/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-aml) · ★116 · 2 copies)
- **anfra-aql**: Core AQL (Analytic Query Language) knowledge — syntax, semantics, functions, worked examples, and gotchas. Use whenever you need to write, read, or debug AQL yourself. ([holistics/anfra](https://github.com/holistics/anfra/tree/HEAD/skills/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-aql) · ★116 · 2 copies)
- **anfra-build-data-app**: Build or change an Anfra Data App — one self-contained HTML file in the repo's `apps/` that queries datasets, and can trace fields' lineage, through the `Anfra` SDK global and renders them with its own HTML, CSS and JS — then check it in `anfra serve`. Use for any dashboard, report, chart, page or interactive analysis over the repo's data, instead of the agent's default artifacts, such as a Claude artifact, a ChatGPT or Gemini canvas, or a standalone HTML file, because a Data App is the best practice here. It gives live data, governed queries, traceability, built-in interactions, and analytic… ([holistics/anfra](https://github.com/holistics/anfra/tree/HEAD/skills/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-build-data-app) · ★116 · 2 copies)
- **anfra-convert-looker**: Convert LookML (views, explores, model files, manifest) into Holistics AML models and datasets with AQL. Use when migrating a Looker project to Holistics or translating a LookML file or snippet into AML. ([holistics/anfra](https://github.com/holistics/anfra/tree/HEAD/skills/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-convert-looker) · ★116 · 2 copies)
- **anfra-docs**: Look up the anfra and AMQL docs — concepts, guides and reference for AML, AQL, modeling patterns, joins, the anfra CLI, the SDK, Data Apps and the semantic catalog. Use when the other anfra skills don't cover what you need, or to check how something in anfra or AMQL works rather than guessing. ([holistics/anfra](https://github.com/holistics/anfra/tree/HEAD/skills/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-docs) · ★116 · 2 copies)
- **anfra-interaction-patterns**: Implements analytical interactions in anfra Data Apps with the @holistics/anfra-sdk: date filters, date drill, cross-filtering, view underlying data, drill down, Top N, period comparison, what-if and 46 more, each with its AQL, SDK wiring and a working example. Use when building or changing an interaction in an anfra Data App, or when told to follow an example from anfra.ai/interaction-patterns. ([holistics/anfra-app-examples](https://github.com/holistics/anfra-app-examples/tree/HEAD/projects/interaction-patterns/skill))
- **anfra-lookup-values**: Verify the exact stored values of a field before filtering on them, guarding against case sensitivity, abbreviations, and typos. Use before writing any filter whose literal values you aren't 100% sure match the data. ([holistics/anfra](https://github.com/holistics/anfra/tree/HEAD/skills/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-lookup-values) · ★116 · 2 copies)
- **anfra-publish**: Publish a repo to its org, and work alongside the others who publish to it — pull their changes, resolve conflicts, and check where this folder stands. Use when the user asks to publish, pull, or sync with their org; when `anfra publish` refuses with `pull_required` or `conflicts_unresolved`; when starting work in a repo that has an org; or to find out whether the user's work is published. ([holistics/anfra](https://github.com/holistics/anfra/tree/HEAD/skills/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-publish) · ★116 · 2 copies)
- **anfra-read-logs**: Read anfra's log to find out why something went wrong, such as a query that ran without the cache, a sidecar that didn't start or stopped answering, or a Data App's runtime errors. Use when an error or hint says to run `anfra logs`, or when something misbehaved and its answer didn't say why. ([holistics/anfra](https://github.com/holistics/anfra/tree/HEAD/skills/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-read-logs) · ★116 · 2 copies)
- **anfra-run-aql**: Run an AQL query against a dataset and return its result rows (or compile it to SQL). Use to execute a validated AQL query and get data. ([holistics/anfra](https://github.com/holistics/anfra/tree/HEAD/skills/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-run-aql) · ★116 · 2 copies)
- **anfra-setup-repo**: Set up a new Anfra repo — install the anfra CLI if needed, run `anfra init`, connect a data source, and get a first model and dataset validated and queried. Use when starting an Anfra project, connecting a database, or when a repo has no `.anfra/` config yet. ([holistics/anfra](https://github.com/holistics/anfra/tree/HEAD/skills/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-setup-repo) · ★116 · 2 copies)
- **anfra-validate-aql**: Validate an AQL query (or a single expression) without running it. Use to type-check AQL before running or presenting it. ([holistics/anfra](https://github.com/holistics/anfra/tree/HEAD/skills/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-validate-aql) · ★116 · 2 copies)
- **anfra-write-aql**: Write AQL queries to answer data questions. Use whenever the user asks for data, wants to query a dataset, needs to filter/aggregate/analyze data, or asks about metrics and dimensions in AMQL.
